```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_budget = min(float(budget_s)-10, 1780)
    best_a = None; best_score = inf
    # load parent
    # if none, default uniform 4096
    # quick candidate generation lengths [1000,2048,4096,8192]
    # for each sample parametric candidates, score, keep top few.
    # combine with parent/interpolated.
    # Sort candidates by score.
    # Allocate time to top candidates: run mirror_soft with time budget based on rank.
    # Periodically final polish with best.
    # return best list.
```

- Ensure final 30-60s for validation and return. Use 1750s.

   For candidate in top list:
      remaining = budget - elapsed - 60
      if remaining < 30 break
      time_alloc = min(remaining*0.4? or fixed 200) maybe top gets 400, second 200, third 100. Or dynamic: always give 15-25% remaining. To maximize, top get large.
      a_out, score_out = smooth_mirror_descent(cand, time_alloc, seed)

Within mirror descent:
```
n=len(a); a=safe_norm(a0)
best_a=a; c=conv_sq(a); best_M=c.max(); best_score=2*n*best_M
u = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
lr = base_step
beta_B = beta_min # B scaling
stall=0
it=0
while time < limit:
  it +=1
  c=conv_sq(a)
  M=c.max()
  if M < best_M - 1e-14: update
  # choose weights
  if M>0:
     z = beta_B*(c-M)/M
  else: break
  z=np.clip(z, -80, 0) # since c-M<=0
  p=np.exp(z)
  s=p.sum()
  if s<=0: p uniform?
  p/=s
  # maybe mix with flat weights
  p_flat = np.maximum(c - 1.0/L? avg,0) # or c - 0.8*avg
  s2=p_flat.sum()
  if s2>0: p_flat/=s2 else p_flat=p
  p = (1-mix)*p + mix*p_flat
  p/=p.sum()
  d = cross_norm(c? p,a)
  dmax=d.max()
  if dmax<=0: perturb
  direction = d / dmax
  # try step sizes
  accepted=False
  for step in [lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25]:
      a_try = a * np.exp(-step * direction)
      a_try = safe_norm(a_try)
      c_try=conv_sq(a_try); M_try=c_try.max()
      # Also if M_try < M - tol or (M_try < M and maybe smooth?) accept
      if M_try < M - 1e-15:
          a=a_try; M=M_try; lr = min(lr*1.1, max_step) maybe; accepted=True; break
  if not accepted:
      # maybe accept if a_try improved smooth? but skip
      lr *= 0.7
      if not improved for many: betas, restarts
  it +=1
```
This recomputes conv for each trial; maybe too many. To reduce, just accept if M_try < M (best current or some smooth). We can accept even if not improving best? To traverse valley, need accept slight worsens? Usually local descent for nonconvex shouldn't accept worse. We can use best-first: current a may worsen, best kept. If no improvement after some, do perturbation from best.

Maybe use "simulated annealing"? Accept occasional worse to escape, but final best. Could do small probability. But score target precise; deterministic improvements likely.

Soft weights function with q:
```
def compute_weights(c, q, B, mix):
   c = np.maximum(c, 0)
   M = c.max()
   if M <= 0: return uniform
   ratio = c / M
   # power with threshold maybe t=0.2
   # w1 = (ratio ** q)
   # To avoid ignoring low, add uniform eps
   w1 = ratio ** q
   # top softmax
   z = np.clip(B * (ratio - 1.0), -50, 0)  # because ratio-1<=0; B scale
   w2 = np.exp(z); w2/=sum
   if q < 1.5: p = 0.7*w1 + 0.3*w2? Wait ratio^1 weight all. For q=1, ratio^1. mix top 0.1.
   else: mix_top = min(0.7, 0.1 + 0.02*q?)
   p = (1-mix_top)*w1 + mix_top*w2 + eps
```

```
def local_search(a0, time_limit, seed=1, q_init=1.0, q_max=80.0, base_step=1.0, verbose=False):
    n=len(a0); a=safe_norm(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=conv_sq(a).max(); best_score=2*n*best_M
    t0=time.monotonic(); rng=default_rng(seed)
    q = q_init
    B = 5.0
    step_scale = 1.0
    it=0; it_since_best=0; it_since_reach=0
    max_no_improve = 300
    max_no_accept = 800
    current_M=best_M
    velocity used? maybe no.
    while time < limit:
       it+=1; it_since_best+=1
       c=conv_sq(a); M=c.max()
       if M < best_M - 1e-14:
           best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); it_since_best=0; if verbose print
       else:
           current_M=M
       # choose weights
       weights, M = compute_weights(c, q, B)
       d = cross_grad(weights,a)
       dmax=d.max()
       if dmax <= 1e-300:
           # perturb
           a = safe_norm(a + 1e-6/n * rng.normal(0,1,n))
           continue
       unit = d / dmax
       mean_u = unit.mean()
       mean_a = 1.0/n
       # candidate list
       best_trial = None; best_trial_M = M_best_trial
       add_steps = [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0] * step_scale
       for st in add_steps:
           h = st * mean_a * (mean_u - unit)
           a_t = a + h
           if a_t.min()<0: a_t=np.maximum(a_t,0)
           s=a_t.sum(); if s<=0 continue; a_t/=s
           c_t=conv_sq(a_t); M_t=c_t.max()
           if M_t < best_trial_M: best_trial...
       # optionally mult step if many zeros? floor: if support_count < 0.5n:
       supp = (a > 10*a_mean).mean()
       if supp < 0.7:
          floor = 1e-7 # maybe too large? Use 1e-10?
          mult_steps=[0.2,0.5]*step_scale? But too many conv. Maybe skip or one.
       # If best_trial_M < M - 1e-14: accept
       if best_trial_M < M - 1e-14:
           a=best_trial; current_M=best_trial_M; step_scale=min(step_scale*1.05,4.0)
           it_since_accept? maybe
       else:
           # no improvement: try smaller steps? maybe if best_trial close, choose smallest? We can use stored candidate with minimal M and if M - best_trial_M < tol? no.
           # reduce step
           step_scale = max(0.5*step_scale, 0.05)
           # if no accept many, perturb q
           if it_since_accept > 200:
               q = min(q*1.5, q_max)
           if it_since_accept > 800:
               # restart from best with perturbation
               a = perturbed_from_best(best_a, rng, strength=0.05)
               current_M = conv_sq(a).max()
               step_scale = 0.5; q=1.0? maybe adapt
               it_since_accept=0; it_since_best? maybe not
       # Adapt q based on peaks: If actual M close to avg*target? maybe if broad, lower q; if stuck top, raise.
       # Also reduce B? B for softmax top; if q low ignore.
       if it % 500 == 0 and verbose: print
```

def parametric_sequences(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=[]
    # uniform
    out.append(ones)
    # linear/ramp
    for slope in [-3,-1.5,-0.5,0.5,1.5,3]: f=1+slope*(x-0.5); out norm if min>=0
    # quadratic asym: (x-c)^2? endpoints
    for c in [0.25,0.5,0.75], width etc? We'll include exponentials.
    for r in [-6,-3,-1,1,3,6]: exp(r*x)
    # beta asym x^a*(1-x)^b; if a,b in [0,0.2,0.5,1,2]
```

Let's verify gradient cross-correlation formula with FFT using rfft. I want to be absolutely sure. Let's prove with DFT. Let N. Let A[k]=∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j e^{-2π i j k/N}. Let P[k]=∑_{m=0}^{L-1} p_m e^{-2π i m k/N}. Product P[k] * conj(A[k]) = P[k] * ∑_j a_j e^{+2π i j k/N} = ∑_j a_j P[k] e^{+...j}. Inverse DFT: (1/N)∑_k product e^{+2π i i k/N} = ∑_j a_j (1/N)∑_k P[k] e^{2π i (i+j)k/N} * P? Need P expansion: P[k]=∑_m p_m e^{-2π i m k/N}. Then product =∑_m,j p_m a_j e^{2π i (j-m)k/N}. Inverse gives ∑_m,j p_m a_j δ_{i + j - m ≡0 mod N} =∑_m p_m a_j with m = i+j mod N. Thus output at i =∑_m p_m a_{m-i mod N}. We need with a outside zero. If N>2n-2, for i 0..n-1, m 0..L-1, m-i+n? If m-i in [0,n-1], matches. If m-i negative, m-i ≡ N+m-i. Since N+m-i > N-1? Need > L-1? Actually N > 2n-2, m-i ≥ -(n-1), so mod N≥N-n+1. Since L=2n-1. Is N-n+1 ≥ L? Not necessarily. Need N > 2n-2 means N-n+1 > n-1, but L=2n-1, so N-n+1 could be less than L (e.g., N=2n, N-n+1=n+1 <2n-1). B array has zeros for indices n..N-1 because a only 0..n-1. In output formula, j must be one of the actual a indices 0..n-1; mod equality might have also j = N + m - i not in 0..n-1, so no term. Good. For m-i positive ≥n, j=m-i not in 0..n-1, no term. Compute weights function top-level:
```
def make_weights(c, q, B):
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max())
    L = c.size
    if M <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(L, 1.0/L), M
    ratio = c / M
    # power
    # for q small (1) ratio^1
    if q <= 1.01:
        w1 = ratio.copy()
    else:
        # To avoid underflow, use exp(q*log(ratio+eps))
        w1 = np.exp(np.clip(q*np.log(np.maximum(ratio,1e-300)), -700, 0))
    s1 = w1.sum()
    if s1 <= 1e-300: w1 = ratio?; normalize
    else w1/=s1
    # top softmax: z = B*(ratio-1). Since ratio<=1
    z = B*np.log? Actually ratio-1 <=0; use z=B*(ratio-1). If B=50 and ratio~0.5, z=-25 okay. Clip -50.
    z = np.clip(B*(ratio-1.0), -50.0, 0.0)
    w2 = np.exp(z); s2=w2.sum(); if s2>0 else uniform
    # mix: want more top as q high
    mix_top = 0.0 if q >= 20? Wait if q high, w1 top already. But top softmax smoother. Use mix = min(0.7, 0.1 + 0.015*q) maybe.
    p = (1-mix)*w1 + mix*w2
    p = p + 1e-9
    p //= sum
    return p, M
```
If q=1 and ratio triangular, w1 normalized; mix 1.15? 0.1+0.015=0.115. Good. If q=10, mix 0.25. If q=100 clipped q_max 80, mix 0.7. Good.

But for fine tuning near optimum, maybe pure top? q=80 mix 0.7 top softmax B=50; top weights smoother than power (power zero for ratio 0.9^80=0.0002, top 0.05). Good.

Perturb function:
```
def perturb_sequence(best, strength, rng, n):
   # mixture with random smooth template and/or noise
   a=best.copy()
   # noise relative
   a = a * (1 + strength*rng.normal(0,1,n))
   a = np.maximum(a,0)
   # add small uniform to allow reentry
   a = a + 0.05*strength/n
   return safe_norm(a)
```
strength 0.05-0.2.

Coordinate stir in local_search after long stall:
```
def random_polish(a, time_limit, rng, best_score, seed):
   current = safe_norm(a); M=conv...
   t0=time
   while time < limit:
      best_M_trial = M
      # batch of e.g. 20 random moves
      for _ in range(20):
         src = choose weighted by a? Use rng.random(n) < a*? Use random index with a > threshold
         j = rng.integers(n)
         frac = rng.uniform(0.001,0.05)
         Δ = a[src]*frac
         if Δ<=0 continue
         a_t=a.copy(); a_t[src]-=Δ; a_t[j]+=Δ; norm
         c_t=conv; M_t=max
         if M_t < best_M_trial: best_trial=a_t,best_M=M_t,best_score=2nM_t
      if best_M < M - tol:
          a=best_trial; M=best_M
      else:
          if no improve for several batches: break
   return a,best_score
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def norm_pos(a): ...
def fft_len(n): size=2*n-1; return 1 << (size-1).bit_length()

def conv_sq(a):
    n=len(a); N=fft_len(n); A=np.fft.rfft(a.astype(np.float64), N); C=A*A; c=np.fft.irfft(C,N)[: (2*n-1)]
    c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    # fix sum? not needed
    return c

def score_of(a):
    n=len(a); c=conv_sq(a); return float(2.0*n*c.max())

def evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
       arr=[float(x) for x in a]
       val=evaluate_sequence(arr)
       return float(val)
    except Exception:
       return float('inf')

def make_weights(c, q, B=10.0):
    L=c.shape[0]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    M=float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(L,1.0/L), M
    ratio=c/M
    # power w1
    if q <= 1.05: w1=ratio.copy()
    else:
       # clip ratio min 1e-300
       w1=np.exp(np.clip(q*np.log(np.maximum(ratio,1e-300)), -700.0, 0.0))
    s1=w1.sum()
    if s1 <= 1e-300:
       w1=np.full(L,1.0/L)
    else: w1/=s1
    # softmax top
    z=np.clip(B*(ratio-1.0), -60.0, 0.0)
    w2=np.exp(z)
    s2=w2.sum()
    if s2 <= 1e-300: w2=np.full(L,1.0/L)
    else: w2/=s2
    mix=0.15 + 0.012*q
    if mix>0.6: mix=0.6
    p=(1.0-mix)*w1 + mix*w2 + 1e-10
    sp=p.sum()
    if sp<=0: p=np.full(L,1.0/L)
    else: p/=sp
    return p,M

def cross_gradient(p,a):
    n=len(a); L=len(p); N=1 << (L+n-2).bit_length()? Wait L=2n-1, L+n-2=3n-3. Need N >2n-2 only? Earlier formula said N > 2n-2. But in convolution/correlation, p length L, a length n. The output length L+n-1 for linear cross-corr. To avoid alias, N must be > max index difference? We determined N ≥ L=2n-1? Let's re-evaluate with DFT formula using A nonzero 0..n-1, P nonzero 0..L-1. Output i =∑ p_m a_{m-i mod N}. Need congruence m - i - j ≡0 for j in 0..n-1. |m-i-j| within? m up to L-1=2n-2, i 0..n-1, j 0..n-1. Max m-i-j: 2n-2 -0 -0 =2n-2. Min: 0 - (n-1) - (n-1)= -2n+2. Need N > 2n-2 so congruence not wrap. Thus N ≥2n-1 works. It doesn't depend on L+n. Because we're evaluating only i range and a zero outside. Good. Use N=fft_len(n) = pof2>=2n-1.
    Ahat = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    Phat = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    d=np.fft.irfft(Phat * np.conjugate(Ahat), N)[:n]
    d = np.maximum(d.real,0.0)
    return d
```

def random_template(n, rng):
    t = int(rng.integers(5))
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if t==0: f=np.ones(n)
    elif t==1: f=np.exp(rng.uniform(-5,5)*x)
    elif t==2: a,b = rng.uniform(0,2,2); f=x**a*(1-x)**b +1e-6? If expponents 0 okay x^0=1. If a or b 0, at0/1? Use np.where.
    elif t==3: f=1 + rng.uniform(-0.9,0.9)*np.sin(np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + ...
    elif t==4: gaussian bump
    f=np.maximum(f,0)+1e-12; return norm_pos(f)
```

Perturb:
```
def perturb_sequence(base, strength, rng):
    n=len(base)
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    a=base.copy()
    if strength <=0: return a
    r = int(rng.integers(3))
    if r==0 or r==1:
       a = a * (1.0 + strength*rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
    else:
       a = a * (1.0 + strength*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n))
       a = a + strength*0.5/n * np.ones(n)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    # maybe mix template
    if rng.random() < 0.4:
       tmpl=random_template(n,rng)
       w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3*max(1,strength*10?)))
       a = (1-w)*a + w*tmpl
    return norm_pos(a)
```
But strength 0.1, mixing 0.3 okay. For restart large strength 0.5.

def parametric_candidates(n, rng, seed?):

    x0=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    out=[]

    # asym exp

    # beta asym
    for a in [0.2,0.5,1,2]: for b in [0.2,0.5,1,2]: f=(x+1e-6)**a * (1-x+1e-6)**b; out.append(norm_pos(f))
    # sinusoids
    for phase in [0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2]:
       for amp in [0.5,0.8]:
          f=1+amp*np.sin(np.pi*(x)+phase) maybe normalize; also 2π.
          out.append
    # triangle/ramp
    for c in [0.25,0.75]: f=np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/0.05) + 0.3*np.exp(-((x-1-c)**2)/0.05)?
    limit perhaps 60.
    return out

Main `local_search` detailed code:
```
def local_search(a0, time_limit, seed=0, q_init=1.0, verbose=False):
    n=len(a0)
    a=norm_pos(a0)
    c=conv_sq(a); current_M=float(c.max()); best_M=current_M; best_a=a.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    q=q_init; B=8.0; abs_scale=1.0; rel_scale=1.0
    stall=0; accept_best=0; it=0; worst_worsen=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it+=1
       c=conv_sq(a); current_M=float(c.max())
       if current_M < best_M - 1e-14:
           best_M=current_M; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
           if verbose print
           # on new best, maybe reduce q gradually? keep; maybe lower q to broaden if improved a lot
           if q > 1.0: q = max(1.0, q*0.9)
       else:
           stall += 1
       # if time left low, break? check after conv
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit - 0.1: break
       # weights
       p, Mw = make_weights(c, q, B)
       d = cross_gradient(p,a)
       dmax=float(d.max())
       if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
          a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.05, rng); c=conv_sq(a); continue
       unit = d / dmax
       mean_u = float(unit.mean())
       mean_a = 1.0/n
       # candidate generation and evaluation
       best_trial_M = current_M
       best_trial_a = None
       # abs steps
       for st in (0.3,0.7,1.4)?:
          h = st*abs_scale*mean_a*(mean_u - unit)
          a_t = a + h
          a_t = np.maximum(a_t,0)
          s = a_t.sum()
          if s <= 1e-300: continue
          a_t = a_t/s
          c_t = conv_sq(a_t); Mt=float(c_t.max())
          if Mt < best_trial_M:
             best_trial_M=Mt; best_trial_a=a_t
          if time over... break
       # rel steps
       for st in (0.05,0.1,0.2): maybe scale rel_scale
          factor = 1.0 - st*rel_scale*(unit - mean_u)
          a_t = a * factor + 1e-12 # floor?
          np.maximum, normalize
          evaluate
       # if stall > 200, random transfer
       if stall > 200:
          src = ...; fr=...; evaluate
       # decide accept
       if best_trial_a is not None and best_trial_M < current_M - 1e-15:
           a=best_trial_a; current_M=best_trial_M; abs_scale=min(abs_scale*1.1,3.0); rel_scale=min(...)
           stall_accept=0
       elif best_trial_a is not None and best_trial_M < current_M + 1e-13:
           a=best_trial_a; current_M=best_trial_M
       else:
           # maybe if best_trial_M < current_M + some tiny accept? else reject
           # reduce scales
           abs_scale=max(0.6*abs_scale,0.05); rel_scale=max(0.6*rel_scale,0.02)
           if stall > 300:
              q = min(q*1.5, 80.0); B=min(B+1,30)
           if stall > 1000:
              # restart from best with perturbation
              strength = 0.05 + 0.05*((it%...))
              a = perturb_sequence(best_a, strength, rng)
              c = conv_sq(a); current_M = float(c.max())
              abs_scale=0.5; rel_scale=0.2; q=1.0; stall=0?
           if stall > 5000:
              # large reset
              a = random_template(n,rng) maybe mix best 0.5
              current_M=...
              q=1.0; abs_scale=0.5
       # If current_M > best_M *1.02, reset to best perturb small
       if current_M > best_M * 1.02 + 1e-16:
          a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.02, rng); current_M=conv_sq(a).max(); ...
```

In loop we have unit. Let's implement gradient-informed random move for stall:
```
src_weights = a * unit
src = rng.choice(n, p=src_weights/src.sum())
dest_weights = np.maximum(1.0 - unit, 1e-8)
dest = rng.choice(n, p=dest_weights/dest_weights.sum())
if src==dest: continue
frac = float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.05))
Δ = a[src]*frac
if Δ <= 0: continue
a_t = a.copy(); a_t[src]-=Δ; a_t[dest]+=Δ; normalize
evaluate
```
This may transfer from high penalty to low penalty. More effective than random.

```
def base_shapes(n, rng):
    out=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # uniform
    out.append(ones)
    # exponential slopes
    for r in [-6,-2,-1,1,2,6]: out.append(norm_pos(np.exp(r*x)))
    # beta asym
    for a in [0.3,0.7,1.2]: for b in [0.3,0.7,1.2]: out.append(norm_pos((x+1e-6)**a * (1-x+1e-6)**b))
    # sinusoid random phases
    for _ in range(6):
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.9))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       f=1 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*x + ph)
       out.append(norm_pos(f))
    # gaussian bump random center width
    for _ in range(6):
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.9)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
       f=np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w*w)) + 0.2
       # maybe two bumps
       out.append(norm_pos(f))
    return out
```
Limit total ~ 1 +6+9+6+6=28 per n. Good. If 3 n ->84. Fast.

Interpolation function:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f=norm_pos(f)
    old=len(f)
    if old==new_n: return f.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,f)
    return norm_pos(y)
```

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def norm_pos(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s
```
If a empty? return zeros? But sequences nonempty. For interp new_n maybe >0.

`fft_len`:
```
def fft_len(n):
    size = 2*n - 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
```

`conv_sq`:
```
def conv_sq(a):
   n=int(len(a)); N=fft_len(n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a.astype(np.float64), N)
   C=A*A
   c=np.fft.irfft(C, N)[: (2*n-1)]
   c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
   # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
   return c
```
Potential issue: If N not large enough? pof2 size. Good.

`fast_M`:
```
def fast_M(a):
   c=conv_sq(a); m=float(c.max()); return m if np.isfinite(m) else inf
```

`score_seq`: `2*len(a)*fast_M(a)`.

def evaluate_safe(a):
   try:
       arr=[float(x) for x in np.asarray(a).ravel().tolist()]
       val=evaluate_sequence(arr)
       return float(val)
   except Exception:
       return float('inf')
```

`base_shapes(n, rng, max_count=30)`. Need use `rng.random`, `rng.integers`, `rng.uniform`.
```
def base_shapes(n, rng, max_count=30):
    out=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    xe=x+1e-6
    one=1.0/n; out.append(np.full(n, one))
    if len(out) >= max_count: return out
    for r in [-6,-3,-1,1,3,6]:
       f=np.exp(r*x)
       out.append(norm_pos(f))
       if len(out)>=max_count: break
    for a_exp in (0.3,0.7,1.3):
       for b_exp in (0.3,0.7,1.3):
          f=xe**a_exp * (1.0-xe)**b_exp + 1e-8
          out.append(norm_pos(f))
          if len(out)>=max_count: break
       if len(out)>=max_count: break
    for _ in range(6):
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.9))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi))
       f=1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*x + ph)
       out.append(norm_pos(f))
    for _ in range(6):
       c1=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.9)); w1=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
       f=np.exp(-((x-c1)**2)/(2*w1*w1))
       if rng.random()<0.5:
          c2=1.0-c1; w2=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
          f += 0.5*np.exp(-((x-c2)**2)/(2*w2*w2))
       f += 0.05
       out.append(norm_pos(f))
    return out
```
Need if a_exp/b_exp 0? not, use 0.3 etc.

`random_template(n, rng)` similar one.

`perturb_sequence(base, strength, rng)`:
```
def perturb_sequence(base, strength, rng):
    a=norm_pos(base).copy()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a
    if not np.isfinite(strength) or strength <= 0.0:
        return a
    # noise
    a = a * (1.0 + strength * rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n))
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
       tmpl=random_template(n,rng)
       w=float(rng.uniform(0.05, min(0.4, 0.1+strength*0.8)))
       a=(1.0-w)*a + w*tmpl
    a = a + max(1e-12, 0.01*strength)/max(1,n)? Actually absolute floor:
    a = a + 1e-10
    return norm_pos(a)
```
Add floor constant e.g. 1e-9? To keep zeros reenter. For n=5000, 1e-9 sum5e-6 negligible. Use 1e-9.

Detailed local_search code:
```
def local_search(a0, time_limit, seed=0, q_init=1.0, verbose=False):
    a = norm_pos(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2:
       return a.copy(), float('inf')? If n=1, score? But not.
    c=conv_sq(a)
    current_M=float(c.max()); best_M=current_M; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=2*n*best_M
    t0=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    q=max(1.0, float(q_init))
    B=8.0
    abs_scale=0.8
    rel_scale=0.2
    max_abs_scale=2.5
    max_rel_scale=0.8
    stall_accept=0
    stall_best=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
       it += 1
       # recompute c (could reuse previous? after accept we have c, but simpler)
       c=conv_sq(a)
       current_M=float(c.max())
       if current_M < best_M - 1e-14:
           best_M=current_M
           best_a=a.copy()
           stall_best=0
           if verbose:
              if it % 100 == 0 or best_score - 2*n*best_M > 1e-7:
                  print(f"  local[{it}] M={best_M:.10g} score={2*n*best_M:.8f}")
           # On improvement, maybe slightly broaden
           if q > 1.0 and it % 20 == 0:
               q = max(1.0, q*0.9)
       else:
           stall_best = stall_best + 1
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
       if elapsed > time_limit - 0.5:
           break
       # weights and direction
       p, Mw = make_weights(c, q, B)
       d = cross_gradient(p, a)
       dmax=float(d.max())
       if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
           a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.05, rng)
           continue
       unit = d / dmax
       # if nan in unit, fallback
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(unit)):
           a = perturb_sequence(best_a,0.05,rng); continue
       mean_u = float(unit.mean())
       mean_a = 1.0 / n
       best_trial_M = current_M
       best_trial_a = None
       # abs candidates
       for st in (0.4, 1.0):
           if elapsed > time_limit - 0.5: break
           h = st * abs_scale * mean_a * (mean_u - unit)
           a_t = a + h
           if a_t.min() < 0:
               a_t = np.maximum(a_t, 0.0)
           s = float(a_t.sum())
           if s > 1e-300:
               a_t = a_t / s
               ct = conv_sq(a_t)
               Mt = float(ct.max())
               if np.isfinite(Mt) and Mt < best_trial_M:
                    best_trial_M = Mt; best_trial_a = a_t
       # rel candidates
       for st in (0.1, 0.3):
           if elapsed > time_limit - 0.5: break
           factor = 1.0 - st * rel_scale * (unit - mean_u)
           # factor can be negative for large; set min 0?
           if factor.min() < 0.0:
               factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.0)
           a_t = a * factor + 1e-12
           s = float(a_t.sum())
           if s > 1e-300:
               a_t = a_t / s
               ct = conv_sq(a_t); Mt=float(ct.max())
               if np.isfinite(Mt) and Mt < best_trial_M:
                    best_trial_M=Mt; best_trial_a=a_t
       # random gradient-informed move if stall
       if stall_accept > 80 or stall_best > 400:
           src_w = a * unit
           sw = float(src_w.sum())
           if sw > 1e-300:
               src = int(rng.choice(n, p=src_w/sw))
           else:
               src = int(rng.integers(n))
           dest_w = 1.0 - unit
           dest_w = np.maximum(dest_w, 1e-8)
           dw = float(dest_w.sum())
           dest = int(rng.choice(n, p=dest_w/dw))
           if src != dest and a[src] > 1e-15:
               frac = float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.05))
               delta = a[src] * frac
               a_t = a.copy()
               a_t[src] -= delta
               a_t[dest] += delta
               a_t = np.maximum(a_t, 0.0)
               s = float(a_t.sum())
               if s > 1e-300:
                   a_t /= s
                   ct=conv_sq(a_t); Mt=float(ct.max())
                   if np.isfinite(Mt) and Mt < best_trial_M:
                        best_trial_M=Mt; best_trial_a=a_t
       # accept logic
       accepted=False
       if best_trial_a is not None and best_trial_M < current_M - 1e-15:
           a = best_trial_a
           current_M = best_trial_M
           abs_scale = min(abs_scale*1.05, max_abs_scale)
           rel_scale = min(rel_scale*1.05, max_rel_scale)
           stall_accept=0
           accepted=True
       elif best_trial_a is not None and best_trial_M < current_M + 1e-13:
           a=best_trial_a; current_M=best_trial_M; stall_accept=0; accepted=True
       else:
           abs_scale=max(0.7*abs_scale, 0.05)
           rel_scale=max(0.7*rel_scale, 0.02)
           stall_accept += 1
           if stall_accept > 150:
               q = min(q * 1.4, 80.0)
               B = min(B + 0.5, 30.0)
           if stall_accept > 600:
               # perturb current modestly
               strength = 0.05 + 0.02*float((stall_accept % 200)/200)
               a = perturb_sequence(best_a, strength, rng)
               ct=conv_sq(a); current_M=float(ct.max())
               abs_scale=0.5; rel_scale=0.15; q=max(1.0, q*0.5); B=8.0
               stall_accept=0
           if stall_accept > 2000:
               # larger restart from template mixed
               tmpl=random_template(n,rng)
               w=0.4
               a=norm_pos((1-w)*best_a + w*tmpl)
               ct=conv_sq(a); current_M=float(ct.max())
               abs_scale=0.5; rel_scale=0.2; q=1.0; B=8.0
               stall_accept=0
       # if current much worse than best, pull back
       if current_M > best_M * 1.02 + 1e-16:
           a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.02, rng)
           ct=conv_sq(a); current_M=float(ct.max())
           abs_scale=0.4; rel_scale=0.1; stall_accept=0
    return best_a.copy(), float(2*n*best_M)
```

def top_k(scored,k):
    if not scored: return []
    arr=list(scored)
    res=[]
    for _ in range(k):
       if not arr: break
       best_i=0; best_v=arr[0][0]
       for i in range(1,len(arr)):
          if arr[i][0] < best_v: best_v=arr[i][0]; best_i=i
       res.append(arr.pop(best_i))
    return res
```

def top_scored(scored, k):
    remaining = list(scored)
    res=[]
    for _ in range(k):
        if not remaining: break
        bi=0; bv=remaining[0][0]
        for i in range(1,len(remaining)):
            v=remaining[i][0]
            if v < bv:
                bv=v; bi=i
        res.append(remaining.pop(bi))
    return res
```

Code for `select_initial`:
```
def select_initial(base, time_limit, seed):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    scored=[]
    b=norm_pos(base)
    add_candidate(scored,b)
    lengths=choose_lengths(len(b))
    # Interp base to lengths
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit*0.3: break
       add_candidate(scored, interpolate_sequence(b,n))
    # shifted base
    n0=len(b)
    if n0>3:
       s=np.zeros(n0); s[1:]=b[:-1]; add_candidate(scored,norm_pos(s))
       s=np.zeros(n0); s[:-1]=b[1:]; add_candidate(scored,norm_pos(s))
    # shapes per length
    for n in lengths:
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
       if elapsed > time_limit*0.6: break
       shapes=base_shapes(n, rng, 8)
       for sh in shapes:
          if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit*0.85: break
          add_candidate(scored, sh)
    # small search
    elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
    if elapsed < time_limit*0.9:
       ns=128
       smalls=base_shapes(ns, rng, 8)
       best_s=None; best_sc=float('inf')
       for s in smalls:
           sc=score_seq(s)
           if sc < best_sc:
              best_sc=sc; best_s=s
       if best_s is not None:
           small_time = max(1.0, min(8.0, (time_limit - elapsed)*0.25))
           small_a, _ = local_search(best_s, small_time, seed=seed+11, verbose=False)
           for n in lengths:
              if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
              add_candidate(scored, interpolate_sequence(small_a,n))
              # maybe a shape from small at n? small_a interp enough.
    top=top_scored(scored,3)
    if top:
       return top[0][1], top[0][0]
    return b, score_seq(b)
```

def choose_lengths(n):

But before final, let's sanity check cross_gradient orientation with an example to ensure update correct. Suppose n=3, a=[0.2,0.3,0.5]. p concentrated at lag m=1. Desired gradient for c_1=2a0a1; derivative wrt a0=2a1=0.6, a1=2a0=0.4, a2=0. p=[0,1,0...]. Our cross_gradient p,a: output i =∑ p_m a_{m-i}. For p1, i0 a1=0.3, i1 a0=0.2, i2 a-1=0. Unit [0.3,0.2,0]. Gradient up to factor2 [0.6,0.4,0]. Good. Additive direction `mean_u - unit`; unit normalized to [1,0.667,0], mean0.556. h: i0 1-0.556=0.444 -> decreases a0 (good high gradient), i1 0.667-0.556=0.111 -> decreases a1 less, i2 0-0.556 negative -> increases a2 (zero gradient). This reduces c_1 by moving mass from a0/a1 to a2. Good.

```
"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time

def norm_pos(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size ==0:
       return np.ones(1)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size,dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s

def fft_len(n):
    n=max(int(n),1)
    size=2*n-1
    return 1 << (size-1).bit_length()

def conv_sq(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n == 1:
       v=float(a[0])
       return np.array([v*v])
    N=fft_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a.astype(np.float64), N)
    C=A*A
    c=np.fft.irfft(C, N)[: (2*n-1)]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c

def fast_M(a):
    a=norm_pos(a)
    c=conv_sq(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return float('inf')
    return m

def score_seq(a):
    a=norm_pos(a)
    m=fast_M(a) # again norm? fast_M normalizes; okay but it computes conv maybe twice? We can optimize:
```
Avoid double norm: `fast_M` can assume normalized? But okay. To avoid double, create `M_of_normed(a)`? Simpler: score_seq calls conv_sq directly:

def score_seq(a):
    a=norm_pos(a)
    c=conv_sq(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*len(a)*m)
```

```
def make_weights(c, q, B):
    c=np.maximum(np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
    L=c.size
    M=float(c.max())
    if L==0: return np.array([], dtype=float), 0.0
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(L)/L, M
    ratio=c/M
    if q <= 1.05:
        w1=ratio.copy()
    else:
        safe=np.maximum(ratio,1e-300)
        w1=np.exp(np.clip(q*np.log(safe), -700.0, 0.0))
    s1=float(w1.sum())
    if s1 <=1e-300:
        w1=np.ones(L)/L
    else:
        w1=w1/s1
    z=np.clip(B*(ratio-1.0), -70.0, 0.0)
    w2=np.exp(z)
    s2=float(w2.sum())
    if s2 <=1e-300:
        w2=np.ones(L)/L
    else: w2=w2/s2
    mix=0.10 + 0.012*float(q)
    if mix > 0.6: mix=0.6
    p=(1.0-mix)*w1 + mix*w2 + 1e-10
    sp=float(p.sum())
    if sp <=1e-300: p=np.ones(L)/L
    else: p=p/sp
    return p,M
```
For q=1, B maybe 5; if ratio zeros, z=-B, w2 not huge. mix 0.112.

`cross_gradient`:
```
def cross_gradient(p,a):
    n=len(a)
    L=len(p)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    # N must be >2n-2; use fft_len(n) (>=2n-1)
    N=fft_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64), N)
    P=np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64), N)
    d=np.fft.irfft(P * np.conjugate(A), N)[:n]
    d=np.maximum(d.real,0.0)
    return d
```
If p length L < N, rfft pads. Good.

`interpolate_sequence` as above. It should handle if f empty. Use new_n int.

```
def random_template(n, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    r=int(rng.integers(4))
    if r==0:
       f=np.ones(n)
    elif r==1:
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.9)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
       f=np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w*w)) + 0.2
    elif r==2:
       rmp=float(rng.uniform(-6,6))
       f=np.exp(rmp*x)+1e-6
    else:
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.9)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       f=1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*x+ph)
    return norm_pos(f)
```
For r=2, exp(6) ~403, sum okay. Good.

`perturb_sequence`:
```
def perturb_sequence(base, strength, rng):
    a=norm_pos(base)
    n=a.size
    if strength <=0 or not np.isfinite(strength): return a
    a=a*(1.0 + strength*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n))
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        tmpl=random_template(n,rng)
        w=float(rng.uniform(0.05, min(0.4, 0.1+0.8*strength)))
        a=(1.0-w)*a + w*tmpl
    a=a + 1e-9
    return norm_pos(a)
```
If strength large 0.5, w max 0.4. Good.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2

The optimizer uses FFT-based quadratic convolution, dense soft-max /
power-weighted subgradients, additive and relative simplex line searches,
multi-start lengths and shapes, a small-scale continuous warm-start, and
gradient-informed random transfers.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def norm_pos(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fft_len(n):
    """Power-of-two FFT length sufficient for length-n linear convolution."""
    n = max(int(n), 1)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def conv_sq(a):
    """Full linear convolution a * a via FFT."""
    n = len(a)
    if n == 1:
        v = float(a[0])
        return np.array([v * v], dtype=np.float64)
    N = fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a.astype(np.float64), N)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_seq(a):
    """Fast score for a normalized nonnegative sequence."""
    a = norm_pos(a)
    c = conv_sq(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * m)


def evaluate_safe(a):
    """Call the official evaluator safely."""
    try:
        arr = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().tolist()]
        val = evaluate_sequence(arr)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def make_weights(c, q, B):
    """
    Convolution-lag weights for gradient computation.
    q is a power exponent; B controls a top-peak softmax mixture.
    """
    c = np.maximum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L), M

    ratio = c / M

    if q <= 1.05:
        w1 = ratio.copy()
    else:
        safe_ratio = np.maximum(ratio, 1e-300)
        w1 = np.exp(np.clip(float(q) * np.log(safe_ratio), -700.0, 0.0))

    s1 = float(w1.sum())
    if s1 <= 1e-300:
        w1 = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        w1 = w1 / s1

    z = np.clip(float(B) * (ratio - 1.0), -70.0, 0.0)
    w2 = np.exp(z)
    s2 = float(w2.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        w2 = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        w2 = w2 / s2

    mix = 0.10 + 0.012 * float(q)
    if mix > 0.6:
        mix = 0.6
    p = (1.0 - mix) * w1 + mix * w2 + 1e-10
    sp = float(p.sum())
    if sp <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        p = p / sp
    return p, M


def cross_gradient(p, a):
    """
    Compute g_i = sum_m p_m a_{m-i}.
    This is proportional to the derivative of weighted convolution peaks.
    """
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), N)
    P = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), N)
    d = np.fft.irfft(P * np.conjugate(A), N)[:n]
    d = np.maximum(d.real, 0.0)
    return d


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    """Linearly interpolate a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    f = norm_pos(f)
    old_n = len(f)
    new_n = max(int(new_n), 1)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    if old_n <= 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f)
    return norm_pos(y)


def random_template(n, rng):
    """A simple explicit nonnegative template used for restarts."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(int(n), 1))
    r = int(rng.integers(4))
    if r == 0:
        f = np.ones_like(x)
    elif r == 1:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.5))
        f = np.exp(-((x - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w)) + 0.2
    elif r == 2:
        rmp = float(rng.uniform(-6.0, 6.0))
        f = np.exp(rmp * x) + 1e-6
    else:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        f = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + ph)
    return norm_pos(f)


def perturb_sequence(base, strength, rng):
    """Return a relaxed perturbation of base."""
    a = norm_pos(base)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    strength = float(strength)
    if not np.isfinite(strength) or strength <= 0.0:
        return a
    a = a * (1.0 + strength * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        tmpl = random_template(n, rng)
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, min(0.4, 0.1 + 0.8 * strength)))
        a = (1.0 - w) * a + w * tmpl
    a = a + 1e-9
    return norm_pos(a)


def base_shapes(n, rng, max_count=30):
    """Generate explicit initial shapes."""
    out = []
    n = max(int(n), 1)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out.append(norm_pos(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))

    if len(out) < max_count:
        for r in (-6.0, -3.0, -1.0, 1.0, 3.0, 6.0):
            f = np.exp(r * x)
            out.append(norm_pos(f))
            if len(out) >= max_count:
                break

    if len(out) < max_count:
        xp = x + 1e-6
        x1 = 1.0 - x + 1e-6
        for ae in (0.3, 0.7, 1.3):
            for be in (0.3, 0.7, 1.3):
                f = (xp ** ae) * (x1 ** be) + 1e-8
                out.append(norm_pos(f))
                if len(out) >= max_count:
                    break
            if len(out) >= max_count:
                break

    if len(out) < max_count:
        for _ in range(6):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            f = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + ph)
            out.append(norm_pos(f))
            if len(out) >= max_count:
                break

    if len(out) < max_count:
        for _ in range(6):
            c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9))
            w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.4))
            f = np.exp(-((x - c1) ** 2) / (2.0 * w1 * w1))
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                c2 = 1.0 - c1
                w2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.4))
                f = f + 0.5 * np.exp(-((x - c2) ** 2) / (2.0 * w2 * w2))
            f = f + 0.05
            out.append(norm_pos(f))
            if len(out) >= max_count:
                break

    return out


def choose_lengths(n):
    """Choose a few nearby lengths to explore."""
    n = max(int(n), 1)
    opts = []
    for val in (n, int(1.3 * n), int(0.7 * n), 2048, 4096, 6144, 8192, 1024, 12000):
        val = int(val)
        if val < 256 or val > 12000:
            continue
        if val not in opts:
            opts.append(val)
    return opts[:3]


def add_candidate(scored, a):
    """Score and append a candidate if finite."""
    a = norm_pos(a)
    sc = score_seq(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        scored.append((sc, a))


def top_scored(scored, k):
    """Return up to k entries with smallest scores without lambdas."""
    remaining = list(scored)
    res = []
    for _ in range(k):
        if not remaining:
            break
        bi = 0
        bv = remaining[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(remaining)):
            v = remaining[i][0]
            if v < bv:
                bv = v
                bi = i
        res.append(remaining.pop(bi))
    return res


def local_search(a0, time_limit, seed=0, q_init=1.0, verbose=False):
    """
    Local optimization from a0.
    Returns (best_sequence, best_fft_score).
    """
    a = norm_pos(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 1:
        return norm_pos(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)), float("inf")

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        c = conv_sq(a)
        return a.copy(), float(2.0 * n * c.max())

    c = conv_sq(a)
    current_M = float(c.max())
    best_M = current_M
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_M)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    q = max(1.0, float(q_init))
    B = min(5.0 + 0.2 * q, 40.0)

    abs_scale = 0.8
    rel_scale = 0.2
    max_abs_scale = 2.6
    max_rel_scale = 0.85

    stall_accept = 0
    stall_best = 0
    it = 0

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed > time_limit:
            break

        it += 1

        c = conv_sq(a)
        current_M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(current_M):
            a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.05, rng)
            continue

        if current_M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = current_M
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall_best = 0
            if verbose and (it % 250 == 0 or it < 20):
                print(f"    local_search it={it} score={2.0 * n * best_M:.8f} q={q:.2f}")
            if q > 1.0 and it % 25 == 0:
                q = max(1.0, q * 0.9)
                B = min(5.0 + 0.2 * q, 40.0)
        else:
            stall_best += 1

        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 0.5:
            break

        p, _ = make_weights(c, q, B)
        d = cross_gradient(p, a)
        dmax = float(d.max())
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
            a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.05, rng)
            continue

        unit = d / dmax
        mean_u = float(unit.mean())
        if not np.isfinite(mean_u):
            a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.05, rng)
            continue
        mean_a = 1.0 / float(n)

        best_trial_M = current_M
        best_trial_a = None

        # Additive centered steps: can reintroduce mass at low-gradient places.
        for st in (0.5, 1.5):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 0.5:
                break
            h = st * abs_scale * mean_a * (mean_u - unit)
            a_t = a + h
            if a_t.min() < 0.0:
                a_t = np.maximum(a_t, 0.0)
            s = float(a_t.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                a_t = a_t / s
                ct = conv_sq(a_t)
                Mt = float(ct.max())
                if np.isfinite(Mt) and Mt < best_trial_M:
                    best_trial_M = Mt
                    best_trial_a = a_t

        # Relative multiplicative-like steps.
        for st in (0.15, 0.35):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 0.5:
                break
            factor = 1.0 - st * rel_scale * (unit - mean_u)
            if factor.min() < 0.0:
                factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.0)
            a_t = a * factor + 1e-12
            s = float(a_t.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                a_t = a_t / s
                ct = conv_sq(a_t)
                Mt = float(ct.max())
                if np.isfinite(Mt) and Mt < best_trial_M:
                    best_trial_M = Mt
                    best_trial_a = a_t

        # When stuck, perform a gradient-informed single-transfer move.
        if stall_accept > 80 or stall_best > 500:
            src_w = a * unit
            sw = float(src_w.sum())
            if sw > 1e-300:
                src = int(rng.choice(n, p=src_w / sw))
            else:
                src = int(rng.integers(n))

            dest_w = 1.0 - unit
            dest_w = np.maximum(dest_w, 1e-8)
            dw = float(dest_w.sum())
            if dw > 1e-300:
                dest = int(rng.choice(n, p=dest_w / dw))
            else:
                dest = int(rng.integers(n))

            if src != dest and a[src] > 1e-15:
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.05))
                delta = float(a[src] * frac)
                a_t = a.copy()
                a_t[src] -= delta
                a_t[dest] += delta
                a_t = np.maximum(a_t, 0.0)
                s = float(a_t.sum())
                if s > 1e-300:
                    a_t = a_t / s
                    ct = conv_sq(a_t)
                    Mt = float(ct.max())
                    if np.isfinite(Mt) and Mt < best_trial_M:
                        best_trial_M = Mt
                        best_trial_a = a_t

        accepted = False
        if best_trial_a is not None and best_trial_M < current_M - 1e-15:
            a = best_trial_a
            current_M = best_trial_M
            abs_scale = min(abs_scale * 1.05, max_abs_scale)
            rel_scale = min(rel_scale * 1.05, max_rel_scale)
            stall_accept = 0
            accepted = True
        elif best_trial_a is not None and best_trial_M < current_M + 1e-13:
            a = best_trial_a
            current_M = best_trial_M
            stall_accept = 0
            accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            abs_scale = max(0.7 * abs_scale, 0.05)
            rel_scale = max(0.7 * rel_scale, 0.02)
            stall_accept += 1

            if stall_accept > 160:
                q = min(q * 1.4, 80.0)
                B = min(5.0 + 0.2 * q, 40.0)

            if stall_accept > 700:
                strength = 0.05 + 0.02 * float((stall_accept % 300) / 300.0)
                a = perturb_sequence(best_a, strength, rng)
                ct = conv_sq(a)
                current_M = float(ct.max())
                abs_scale = 0.5
                rel_scale = 0.15
                q = max(1.0, q * 0.5)
                B = min(5.0 + 0.2 * q, 40.0)
                stall_accept = 0

            if stall_accept > 2200:
                tmpl = random_template(n, rng)
                w = 0.4
                a = norm_pos((1.0 - w) * best_a + w * tmpl)
                ct = conv_sq(a)
                current_M = float(ct.max())
                abs_scale = 0.5
                rel_scale = 0.2
                q = 1.0
                B = min(5.0 + 0.2 * q, 40.0)
                stall_accept = 0

        # If current point drifted badly above the local best, pull back.
        if np.isfinite(best_M) and current_M > best_M * 1.02 + 1e-16:
            a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.02, rng)
            ct = conv_sq(a)
            current_M = float(ct.max())
            abs_scale = 0.4
            rel_scale = 0.1
            stall_accept = 0

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        ct = conv_sq(best_a)
        best_score = float(2.0 * n * ct.max())
    return best_a.copy(), best_score


def select_initial(base, time_limit, seed=0):
    """
    Quick multi-start selection: parent, interpolations, explicit shapes,
    and a small continuous-like warm-start.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    scored = []

    b = norm_pos(base)
    add_candidate(scored, b)

    lengths = choose_lengths(len(b))

    if time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        for nlen in lengths:
            add_candidate(scored, interpolate_sequence(b, nlen))

    n0 = len(b)
    if n0 > 3:
        s = np.zeros(n0, dtype=np.float64)
        s[1:] = b[:-1]
        if s.sum() > 0:
            add_candidate(scored, norm_pos(s))
        s = np.zeros(n0, dtype=np.float64)
        s[:-1] = b[1:]
        if s.sum() > 0:
            add_candidate(scored, norm_pos(s))

    if time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit * 0.75:
        for nlen in lengths:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            if elapsed > time_limit * 0.65:
                break
            shapes = base_shapes(nlen, rng, 8)
            for sh in shapes:
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit * 0.85:
                    break
                add_candidate(scored, sh)

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    if time_limit - elapsed > 3.0:
        ns = 128
        smalls = base_shapes(ns, rng, 8)
        best_small = None
        best_small_sc = float("inf")
        for s in smalls:
            sc = score_seq(s)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_small_sc:
                best_small_sc = sc
                best_small = s

        if best_small is not None:
            small_time = max(1.0, min(8.0, (time_limit - elapsed) * 0.30))
            small_a, _ = local_search(best_small, small_time, seed=seed + 11, verbose=False)
            for nlen in lengths:
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                    break
                add_candidate(scored, interpolate_sequence(small_a, nlen))

    top = top_scored(scored, 3)
    if not top:
        top = [(score_seq(b), b)]
    return top


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main search function. Invoked by the harness.
    Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0:
        budget = 1800.0

    total_budget = max(120.0, min(budget - 60.0, 1740.0))

    base = None
    base_score = float("inf")

    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if h is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.ndim != 1:
                arr = arr.ravel()
            arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
            if arr.size > 0:
                base = norm_pos(arr)
                base_score = score_seq(base)
                print(f"Loaded parent: n={base.size}, fft_score={base_score:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if base is None:
        base = norm_pos(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        base_score = score_seq(base)
        print(f"No parent available; uniform fallback n={base.size}, score={base_score:.8f}")

    quick_limit = min(90.0, total_budget * 0.18)
    top_cands = select_initial(base, quick_limit, seed=12345)

    if not top_cands:
        top_cands = [(base_score, base)]

    # Make sure the original parent is represented.
    bs = score_seq(base)
    if np.isfinite(bs):
        top_cands.append((bs, base.copy()))
    best_a, best_score = top_cands[0]
    if base_score < best_score - 1e-15:
        best_a = base
        best_score = base_score

    print(f"Initial best: fft_score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_a)}, t={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s")

    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)
    starts = []
    start_scores = []
    for sc, a in top_cands[:3]:
        starts.append(a.copy())
        start_scores.append(float(sc))

    reserve = 60.0
    elapsed_quick = time.monotonic() - start
    main_time = max(0.0, total_budget - reserve - elapsed_quick)

    chunks = (
        (0.42, 1.0, 101),
        (0.28, 4.0, 202),
        (0.18, 12.0, 303),
        (0.12, 25.0, 404),
    )

    pool = [(float(best_score), best_a.copy())]

    for idx, (frac, q_init, seed) in enumerate(chunks):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        rem = total_budget - elapsed
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

        alloc = min(frac * main_time, rem - 10.0)
        if alloc < 8.0:
            alloc = rem - 10.0
            if alloc < 5.0:
                break

        if idx < len(starts) and (idx == 0 or start_scores[idx] < best_score + 0.015):
            start_a = starts[idx]
            if idx > 0:
                start_a = perturb_sequence(start_a, 0.02, rng)
        else:
            start_a = perturb_sequence(best_a, 0.03 + 0.01 * idx, rng)

        a_out, sc_out = local_search(
            start_a,
            alloc,
            seed=seed + idx,
            q_init=q_init,
            verbose=(idx == 0),
        )

        if np.isfinite(sc_out):
            pool.append((float(sc_out), a_out.copy()))
            if sc_out < best_score - 1e-15:
                best_a = a_out.copy()
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                print(
                    f"  Chunk {idx}: new best fft_score={best_score:.8f}, "
                    f"n={len(best_a)}, t={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s"
                )
        else:
            pool.append((float("inf"), norm_pos(np.ones(len(start_a), dtype=np.float64))))

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        if total_budget - elapsed < 30.0:
            break

    # Final official validation of the best FFT-selected sequence.
    final_a = None
    final_score = float("inf")

    ev = evaluate_safe(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(ev):
        final_a = best_a.copy()
        final_score = float(ev)

    # If time remains, validate one or two other promising pool members.
    top_pool = top_scored(pool, 3)
    for sc, a in top_pool:
        rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - start)
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        aw = norm_pos(a)
        ev2 = evaluate_safe(aw)
        if np.isfinite(ev2) and ev2 < final_score:
            final_score = float(ev2)
            final_a = aw.copy()

    if final_a is None or not np.isfinite(final_score):
        # Robust fallback.
        fb = norm_pos(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        try:
            fb_score = evaluate_safe(fb)
        except Exception:
            fb_score = float("inf")
        final_a = fb
        final_score = fb_score
        print("Using fallback uniform sequence.")

    final_a = norm_pos(final_a)
    try:
        final_score_official = evaluate_safe(final_a)
        if np.isfinite(final_score_official):
            final_score = float(final_score_official)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"FINAL: official_score={final_score:.8f}, n={len(final_a)}, time={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s")
    return [float(x) for x in final_a]
```
